# Clock gallery

1425 clocks (187 periods, 139 families), each in the real game at the most active moment of its period; **orange glass = where the output signal is** (placed only for the picture). Click a picture for its design. Generated by `mctas clock catalog` ([docs/clocks.md](../../docs/clocks.md); the period x family matrix: [coverage.md](coverage.md)).

## Extendable clocks

One card per family: the base design, its period and size for any n, how to extend it, the n range verified in the game (`mctas clock extend-check`); their instances are not repeated in the periods below.
